[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +static void test_t1_ll(void)
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +{
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    uint64_t bad = 0U;
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    uint64_t touched = 0U;
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < T1_LL_CASES; k++)
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t tm[T1_NT];
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t rcc = 0U;
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_Status_t st = t1_ll_case(k);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        t1_diff(tm, &rcc, k_t1_ll[k].running != 0U);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        bool same = (rcc == 0U);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t j = 0U; j < T1_NT; j++)
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            same = same && (tm[j] == 0U);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        if (st != (TimBm_Status_t)k_t1_ll[k].expect)
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1ULL << k;
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        if (!same)
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            touched |= 1ULL << k;
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T1 LL %lu %s: status %u (expected %u), registers and RCC banks unchanged %u", (unsigned long)k,
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            k_t1_ll[k].what, (unsigned)st, (unsigned)k_t1_ll[k].expect, (unsigned)same);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t j = 0U; j < T1_NT; j++)
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_LL_Deinit(k_t1_tims[j]);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_LL_Deinit(TIM13);
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (touched == 0U),
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            "T1 LL CM7 %u refusals: wrong status 0x%08lX%08lX, snapshot changed 0x%08lX%08lX", (unsigned)T1_LL_CASES,
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(bad >> 32), (unsigned long)(bad & 0xFFFFFFFFU), (unsigned long)(touched >> 32),
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(touched & 0xFFFFFFFFU));
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    /* O1: the valid LL call changes exactly CCMR1 and CCR1 of TIM2 */
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t tm[T1_NT];
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t rcc = 0U;
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    const TimLl_OcConfig_t oc = { TIM2, LL_TIM_CHANNEL_CH1, LL_TIM_OCMODE_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   2.165s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(&s_t1_b);
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_Status_t st1 = TIM_LL_ConfigOutput(&oc);
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(&s_t1_a);
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +    t1_diff(tm, &rcc, false);
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t exp_tim2 = (1UL << TST_RI_CCMR1) | (1UL << TST_RI_CCR1);
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t others = tm[0] | tm[2] | tm[3] | tm[4] | tm[5] | rcc;
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st1 == TIM_BM_OK) && (tm[1] == exp_tim2) && (others == 0U),
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +            "T1 LL control (O1): ConfigOutput(TIM2 LL_TIM_CHANNEL_CH1 LL_TIM_OCMODE_PWM1 CCR 100 preload) status %u "
[+   2.166s] [host] diff| +            "changes TIM2 words 0x%07lX (0x%07lX: CCMR1, CCR1), other timers and RCC 0x%lX (0)", (unsigned)st1,
[+   2.168s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)tm[1], (unsigned long)exp_tim2, (unsigned long)others);

[+   2.168s] [host] diff| +}
